import torch
import torch.nn as nn

M = 16
N = 4096
K = 4096

def get_inputs():
    return [torch.randn(M, K, dtype=torch.float16).cuda()]

def get_init_inputs():
    return [K, N]

_cuda_code = r'''
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <ATen/cuda/CUDAContext.h>
#include <c10/cuda/CUDAGuard.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <float.h>
#include <cstdint>

static inline __device__ int8_t float_to_int8_rn(float x) {
    uint32_t dst;
    asm volatile("cvt.rni.sat.s8.f32 %0, %1;" : "=r"(dst) : "f"(x));
    return reinterpret_cast<const int8_t&>(dst);
}

static inline __device__ int32_t float_to_int32_rn(float x) {
    uint32_t dst;
    asm volatile("cvt.rni.sat.s32.f32 %0, %1;" : "=r"(dst) : "f"(x));
    return reinterpret_cast<const int32_t&>(dst);
}

static inline __device__ int8_t int32_to_int8(int32_t x) {
    uint32_t dst;
    asm volatile("cvt.sat.s8.s32 %0, %1;" : "=r"(dst) : "r"(x));
    return reinterpret_cast<const int8_t&>(dst);
}

__device__ inline float warpReduceMax(float val) {
    const unsigned mask = 0xffffffff;
    #pragma unroll
    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
        val = fmaxf(val, __shfl_xor_sync(mask, val, offset));
    }
    return val;
}

__device__ inline float blockReduceMax(float val) {
    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    int wid = threadIdx.x >> 5;
    __shared__ float shared[32];
    val = warpReduceMax(val);
    if (lane == 0) shared[wid] = val;
    __syncthreads();
    if (wid == 0) {
        val = (lane < (blockDim.x >> 5)) ? shared[lane] : 0.0f;
        val = warpReduceMax(val);
        if (lane == 0) shared[0] = val;
    }
    __syncthreads();
    return shared[0];
}

template <typename scalar_t>
__global__ void static_scaled_int8_quant_kernel(
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ input,
    int8_t* __restrict__ output,
    const float* __restrict__ scale_ptr,
    int hidden_size) {

    const int64_t token = blockIdx.x;
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;
    const float inv_scale = 1.0f / (*scale_ptr);

    const int64_t row_off = token * hidden_size;
    const scalar_t* row_in = input + row_off;
    int8_t* row_out = output + row_off;

    for (int idx = tid; idx < hidden_size; idx += nthreads) {
        row_out[idx] = float_to_int8_rn(static_cast<float>(row_in[idx]) * inv_scale);
    }
}

template <typename scalar_t>
__global__ void dynamic_scaled_int8_quant_kernel(
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ input,
    int8_t* __restrict__ output,
    float* __restrict__ scale_out,
    int hidden_size) {

    const int64_t token = blockIdx.x;
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;

    const int64_t row_off = token * hidden_size;
    const scalar_t* row_in = input + row_off;
    int8_t* row_out = output + row_off;

    float tmax = 0.0f;
    for (int idx = tid; idx < hidden_size; idx += nthreads) {
        tmax = fmaxf(tmax, fabsf(static_cast<float>(row_in[idx])));
    }

    float bmax = blockReduceMax(tmax);

    __shared__ float s_absmax;
    if (tid == 0) {
        s_absmax = bmax;
        scale_out[token] = bmax / 127.0f;
    }
    __syncthreads();

    float inv_scale = (s_absmax == 0.0f) ? 0.0f : 127.0f / s_absmax;

    for (int idx = tid; idx < hidden_size; idx += nthreads) {
        row_out[idx] = float_to_int8_rn(static_cast<float>(row_in[idx]) * inv_scale);
    }
}

#define DISPATCH_FLOATING_TYPES(TYPE, ...)                          \
    [&] {                                                           \
        switch (TYPE) {                                             \
            case at::ScalarType::Half: { using scalar_t = at::Half; __VA_ARGS__; break; } \
            case at::ScalarType::Float: { using scalar_t = float; __VA_ARGS__; break; } \
            case at::ScalarType::BFloat16: { using scalar_t = at::BFloat16; __VA_ARGS__; break; } \
            default: TORCH_CHECK(false, "Unsupported scalar type ", TYPE); \
        }                                                           \
    }()

void static_scaled_int8_quant(
    torch::Tensor& out,
    const torch::Tensor& input,
    const torch::Tensor& scale) {
    TORCH_CHECK(input.is_contiguous());
    TORCH_CHECK(out.is_contiguous());
    TORCH_CHECK(scale.numel() == 1);

    int hidden_size = input.size(-1);
    int64_t num_tokens = input.numel() / hidden_size;
    dim3 grid(num_tokens);
    dim3 block(256);
    at::cuda::OptionalCUDAGuard device_guard(input.device());
    cudaStream_t stream = at::cuda::getCurrentCUDAStream();

    DISPATCH_FLOATING_TYPES(input.scalar_type(), {
        static_scaled_int8_quant_kernel<scalar_t><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(
            input.data_ptr<scalar_t>(),
            out.data_ptr<int8_t>(),
            scale.data_ptr<float>(),
            hidden_size);
    });
}

void dynamic_scaled_int8_quant(
    torch::Tensor& out,
    const torch::Tensor& input,
    torch::Tensor& scales) {
    TORCH_CHECK(input.is_contiguous());
    TORCH_CHECK(out.is_contiguous());
    TORCH_CHECK(scales.is_contiguous());

    int hidden_size = input.size(-1);
    int64_t num_tokens = input.numel() / hidden_size;
    dim3 grid(num_tokens);
    dim3 block(256);
    at::cuda::OptionalCUDAGuard device_guard(input.device());
    cudaStream_t stream = at::cuda::getCurrentCUDAStream();

    DISPATCH_FLOATING_TYPES(input.scalar_type(), {
        dynamic_scaled_int8_quant_kernel<scalar_t><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(
            input.data_ptr<scalar_t>(),
            out.data_ptr<int8_t>(),
            scales.data_ptr<float>(),
            hidden_size);
    });
}

PYBIND11_MODULE(TORCH_EXTENSION_NAME, m) {
    m.def("static_scaled_int8_quant", &static_scaled_int8_quant,
          py::arg("out"), py::arg("input"), py::arg("scale"));
    m.def("dynamic_scaled_int8_quant", &dynamic_scaled_int8_quant,
          py::arg("out"), py::arg("input"), py::arg("scales"));
}
'''

quant_ext = torch.utils.cpp_extension.load_inline(
    name="quant_ext_hopper_v8",
    cpp_sources="",
    cuda_sources=_cuda_code,
    extra_cuda_cflags=["-O3"],
    verbose=False,
)

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, K, N):
        super().__init__()
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(K, N, dtype=torch.float16, device="cuda"))

    def forward(self, x):
        w_int8 = torch.empty(self.weight.shape, dtype=torch.int8, device="cuda")
        w_scale = (self.weight.abs().max() / 127.0).tolist()
        w_scale_t = torch.tensor([w_scale], dtype=torch.float32, device="cuda")
        quant_ext.static_scaled_int8_quant(w_int8, self.weight, w_scale_t)

        x_int8 = torch.empty(x.shape, dtype=torch.int8, device=x.device)
        scales = torch.empty(x.size(0), dtype=torch.float32, device=x.device)
        quant_ext.dynamic_scaled_int8_quant(x_int8, x, scales)

        out = torch._int_mm(x_int8, w_int8)
        out = out.to(torch.float32) * scales.unsqueeze(1) * w_scale
        return out.to(torch.float16)
